}

definition{
  name: byteAt,
  parameters: [s : Bytes, i : Nat],
  result: (Option Nat),
  body: (match (prim index s i : (Option UInt8))
    | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [Nat])
    | Option.some b => (ctor Option.some [Nat] (prim to_nat b : Nat))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

definition{
  name: digitsValue,
  parameters: [s : Bytes, i : Nat, acc : Nat, fuel : Nat],
  result: (Option Nat),
  body: (match fuel
    | Nat.zero => (ctor Option.some [Nat] acc)
    | Nat.succ rest => (match (byteAt s i)
      | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [Nat])
      | Option.some d => (if ((d <? 48) || (57 <? d)) then (ctor Option.none [Nat]) else (digitsValue s (i + 1) ((prim multiply acc 10 : Nat) + (prim subtract d 48 : Nat)) rest)))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  recursive_argument: fuel
}

definition{
  name: parseDigits,
  parameters: [s : Bytes],
  result: (Option Nat),
  body: (if ((Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s) =? 0) then (ctor Option.none [Nat]) else (if (18 <? (Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s)) then (ctor Option.none [Nat]) else (match (byteAt s 0)
    | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [Nat])
    | Option.some lead => (if ((lead =? 48) && (1 <? (Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s))) then (ctor Option.none [Nat]) else (digitsValue s 0 0 (Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s)))))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

definition{
  name: parseNumber,
  parameters: [value : Bytes],
  result: (Option Nat),
  body: (parseDigits value),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

definition{
  name: parsePhase,
  parameters: [value : Bytes],
  result: (Option Nat),
  body: (if (prim equal value 0x6472616674696e67 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (phaseDrafting)) else (if (prim equal value 0x636865636b6564 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (phaseChecked)) else (if (prim equal value 0x70726f76696e67 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (phaseProving)) else (if (prim equal value 0x70726f766564 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (phaseProved)) else (if (prim equal value 0x7075626c6973686564 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (phasePublished)) else (ctor Option.none [Nat]))))))
}

definition{
  name: parseEvent,
  parameters: [value : Bytes],
  result: (Option Nat),
  body: (if (prim equal value 0x636865636b : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (eventCheck)) else (if (prim equal value 0x617070726f7665 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (eventApprove)) else (if (prim equal value 0x617474657374 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (eventAttest)) else (if (prim equal value 0x6661696c : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (eventFail)) else (if (prim equal value 0x7075626c697368 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (eventPublish)) else (if (prim equal value 0x65646974 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (eventEdit)) else (if (prim equal value 0x77616974 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (eventWait)) else (if (prim equal value 0x7265776f7264 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (eventReword)) else (ctor Option.none [Nat])))))))))
}

definition{
  name: sliceOf,
  parameters: [s : Bytes, start : Nat, count : Nat],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (match (prim slice s start count : (Option Bytes))
    | Option.none => 0x
    | Option.some part => part)
}

definition{
  name: fieldEnd,
  parameters: [s : Bytes, i : Nat, fuel : Nat],
  result: Nat,
  body: (match fuel
    | Nat.zero => i
    | Nat.succ rest => (match (byteAt s i)
      | Option.none => i
      | Option.some d => (if (d =? 32) then i else (fieldEnd s (i + 1) rest)))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  recursive_argument: fuel
}

definition{
  name: decodeField2,
  parameters: [s : Bytes, start : Nat, v1 : Nat],
  result: (Option Request),
  body: (let e : Nat := (fieldEnd s start (Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s)); (match (parseEvent (sliceOf s start (prim subtract e start : Nat)))
    | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [Request])
    | Option.some v2 => (if (e =? (Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s)) then (ctor Option.some [Request] (record Request {phase := v1, event := v2})) else (ctor Option.none [Request])))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

definition{
  name: decodeField1,
  parameters: [s : Bytes, start : Nat],
  result: (Option Request),
  body: (let e : Nat := (fieldEnd s start (Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s)); (match (parsePhase (sliceOf s start (prim subtract e start : Nat)))
    | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [Request])
    | Option.some v1 => (if (e <? (Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s)) then (decodeField2 s (e + 1) v1) else (ctor Option.none [Request])))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

definition{
  name: parseOrigin,
  parameters: [value : Bytes],
  result: (Option Nat),
  body: (if (prim equal value 0x666f726765 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (originForge)) else (if (prim equal value 0x70726576696577 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (originPreview)) else (if (prim equal value 0x7075626c6973686564 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (originPublished)) else (ctor Option.none [Nat]))))
}

definition{
  name: parseEffect,
  parameters: [value : Bytes],
  result: (Option Nat),
  body: (if (prim equal value 0x73746f7265 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (effectStore)) else (if (prim equal value 0x6665746368 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (effectFetch)) else (if (prim equal value 0x696e666572 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (effectInfer)) else (if (prim equal value 0x6275696c64 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (effectBuild)) else (if (prim equal value 0x6672616d65 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (effectFrame)) else (if (prim equal value 0x7365727665 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (effectServe)) else (ctor Option.none [Nat])))))))
}

definition{
  name: parseFlag,
  parameters: [value : Bytes],
  result: (Option Nat),
  body: (if (prim equal value 0x796573 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] 1) else (if (prim equal value 0x6e6f : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] 0) else (ctor Option.none [Nat])))
}

definition{
  name: decodeGate5,
  parameters: [s : Bytes, start : Nat, origin : Nat, effect : Nat, hasRecord : Nat, granted : Nat],
  result: (Option GateRequest),
  body: (let e : Nat := (fieldEnd s start (Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s)); (match (parseFlag (sliceOf s start (prim subtract e start : Nat)))
    | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [GateRequest])
    | Option.some denied => (if (e =? (Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s)) then (ctor Option.some [GateRequest] (record GateRequest {origin := origin, effect := effect, hasRecord := hasRecord, granted := granted, denied := denied})) else (ctor Option.none [GateRequest])))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

definition{
  name: decodeGate4,
  parameters: [s : Bytes, start : Nat, origin : Nat, effect : Nat, hasRecord : Nat],
  result: (Option GateRequest),
  body: (let e : Nat := (fieldEnd s start (Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s)); (match (parseFlag (sliceOf s start (prim subtract e start : Nat)))
    | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [GateRequest])
    | Option.some granted => (if (e <? (Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s)) then (decodeGate5 s (e + 1) origin effect hasRecord granted) else (ctor Option.none [GateRequest])))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

definition{
  name: decodeGate3,
  parameters: [s : Bytes, start : Nat, origin : Nat, effect : Nat],
  result: (Option GateRequest),
  body: (let e : Nat := (fieldEnd s start (Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s)); (match (parseFlag (sliceOf s start (prim subtract e start : Nat)))
    | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [GateRequest])
    | Option.some hasRecord => (if (e <? (Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s)) then (decodeGate4 s (e + 1) origin effect hasRecord) else (ctor Option.none [GateRequest])))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

definition{
  name: decodeGate2,
  parameters: [s : Bytes, start : Nat, origin : Nat],
  result: (Option GateRequest),
  body: (let e : Nat := (fieldEnd s start (Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s)); (match (parseEffect (sliceOf s start (prim subtract e start : Nat)))
    | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [GateRequest])
    | Option.some effect => (if (e <? (Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s)) then (decodeGate3 s (e + 1) origin effect) else (ctor Option.none [GateRequest])))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

definition{
  name: decodeGate1,
  parameters: [s : Bytes, start : Nat],
  result: (Option GateRequest),
  body: (let e : Nat := (fieldEnd s start (Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s)); (match (parseOrigin (sliceOf s start (prim subtract e start : Nat)))
    | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [GateRequest])
    | Option.some origin => (if (e <? (Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s)) then (decodeGate2 s (e + 1) origin) else (ctor Option.none [GateRequest])))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]

definition{
  name: encodeGate,
  parameters: [code : Nat],
  result: Bytes,

}

definition{
  name: rule1,
  parameters: [request : Request],
  result: Bool,
  body: ((project request event) =? (eventEdit))
}

definition{
  name: rule2,
  parameters: [request : Request],
  result: Bool,
  body: (((project request event) =? (eventCheck)) && ((project request phase) =? (phaseDrafting)))
}

definition{
  name: rule3,
  parameters: [request : Request],
  result: Bool,
  body: (((project request event) =? (eventApprove)) && ((project request phase) =? (phaseChecked)))
}

definition{
  name: rule4,
  parameters: [request : Request],
  result: Bool,
  body: (((project request event) =? (eventAttest)) && ((project request phase) =? (phaseProving)))
}

definition{
  name: rule5,
  parameters: [request : Request],
  result: Bool,
  body: (((project request event) =? (eventFail)) && ((project request phase) =? (phaseProving)))
}

definition{
  name: rule6,
  parameters: [request : Request],
  result: Bool,
  body: (((project request event) =? (eventPublish)) && ((project request phase) =? (phaseProved)))
}

definition{
  name: rule7,
  parameters: [request : Request],
  result: Bool,
  body: ((project request phase) =? (phaseDrafting))
}

definition{
  name: rule8,
  parameters: [request : Request],
  result: Bool,
  body: ((project request phase) =? (phaseChecked))
}

definition{
  name: rule9,
  parameters: [request : Request],
  result: Bool,
  body: ((project request phase) =? (phaseProving))
}

definition{
  name: rule10,
  parameters: [request : Request],
  result: Bool,
  body: ((project request phase) =? (phaseProved))
}

definition{
  name: judge,
  parameters: [request : Request],
  result: Nat,
  body: (if (rule1 request) then (answerDrafting) else (if (rule2 request) then (answerChecked) else (if (rule3 request) then (answerProving) else (if (rule4 request) then (answerProved) else (if (rule5 request) then (answerChecked) else (if (rule6 request) then (answerPublished) else (if (rule7 request) then (answerDrafting) else (if (rule8 request) then (answerChecked) else (if (rule9 request) then (answerProving) else (if (rule10 request) then (answerProved) else (answerPublished)))))))))))
}

definition{
  name: encodeAnswer,
  parameters: [code : Nat],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (if (code =? (answerDrafting)) then 0x6472616674696e67 else (if (code =? (answerChecked)) then 0x636865636b6564 else (if (code =? (answerProving)) then 0x70726f76696e67 else (if (code =? (answerProved)) then 0x70726f766564 else (if (code =? (answerPublished)) then 0x7075626c6973686564 else 0x696e76616c6964)))))
}

definition{
  name: siteBudget,
  parameters: [journey : Nat],
  result: Nat,
  body: (if (journey =? 0) then 11500 else (if (journey =? 1) then 2200 else (if (journey =? 2) then 450 else (if (journey =? 3) then 5000 else (if (journey =? 4) then 615 else (if (journey =? 5) then 930 else (if (journey =? 6) then 645 else (if (journey =? 7) then 645 else (if (journey =? 8) then 630 else (if (journey =? 9) then 945 else (if (journey =? 10) then 960 else 0)))))))))))
}

definition{
  name: buildBudget,
  parameters: [journey : Nat],
  result: Nat,
  body: (if (journey =? 11) then 1999 else (if (journey =? 12) then 19999 else (if (journey =? 13) then 99 else (if (journey =? 14) then 15 else (if (journey =? 15) then 33 else (if (journey =? 16) then 2999 else (if (journey =? 17) then 29999 else 0)))))))
}

definition{
  name: budgetOf,
  parameters: [journey : Nat],
  result: Nat,
  body: (if (journey <? 11) then (siteBudget journey) else (buildBudget journey))
}

definition{
  name: budgetWithin,
  parameters: [request : BudgetRequest],
  result: Nat,
  body: (if ((budgetOf (project request journey)) <? (project request value)) then 0 else 1)
}

definition{

  parameters: [request : LayoutRequest],

}

definition{

  parameters: [request : MotionRequest],
  result: Nat,
  body: (if ((project request reduced) =? 1) then 0 else (if ((motionMaximum) <? (project request duration)) then 0 else 1))
}

  parameters: [request : TargetRequest],

definition{

}

definition{
  name: parseJourney,
  parameters: [value : Bytes],
  result: (Option Nat),
  body: (if (prim equal value 0x636f6c64 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (journeyCold)) else (if (prim equal value 0x7761726d : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (journeyWarm)) else (if (prim equal value 0x696e70 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (journeyInp)) else (if (prim equal value 0x746f6b656e : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (journeyToken)) else (if (prim equal value 0x686f6d65 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (journeyHome)) else (if (prim equal value 0x63686174 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (journeyChat)) else (if (prim equal value 0x73657474696e6773 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (journeySettings)) else (if (prim equal value 0x64657461696c73 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (journeyDetails)) else (if (prim equal value 0x61707073 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (journeyApps)) else (if (prim equal value 0x636f6465 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (journeyCode)) else (if (prim equal value 0x7075626c697368 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (journeyPublish)) else (if (prim equal value 0x70726576696577 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (journeyPreview)) else (if (prim equal value 0x6465636c61726174696f6e : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (journeyDeclaration)) else (if (prim equal value 0x706978656c : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (journeyPixel)) else (if (prim equal value 0x757064617465 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (journeyUpdate)) else (if (prim equal value 0x65646974 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (journeyEdit)) else (if (prim equal value 0x636865636b6564 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (journeyChecked)) else (if (prim equal value 0x6275696c74 : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Nat] (journeyBuilt)) else (ctor Option.none [Nat])))))))))))))))))))
}

definition{
  name: numberTail,
  parameters: [s : Bytes, start : Nat],
  result: (Option Nat),
  body: (let e : Nat := (fieldEnd s start (Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s)); (if (e =? (Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s)) then (parseNumber (sliceOf s start (prim subtract e start : Nat))) else (ctor Option.none [Nat]))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

definition{
  name: flagTail,
  parameters: [s : Bytes, start : Nat],
  result: (Option Nat),
  body: (let e : Nat := (fieldEnd s start (Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s)); (if (e =? (Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s)) then (parseFlag (sliceOf s start (prim subtract e start : Nat))) else (ctor Option.none [Nat]))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

definition{
  name: decodeBudget,
  parameters: [s : Bytes, start : Nat],
  result: (Option BudgetRequest),
  body: (let e : Nat := (fieldEnd s start (Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s)); (match (parseJourney (sliceOf s start (prim subtract e start : Nat)))
    | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [BudgetRequest])
    | Option.some j => (if (e <? (Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s)) then (match (numberTail s (e + 1))
      | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [BudgetRequest])
      | Option.some v => (ctor Option.some [BudgetRequest] (record BudgetRequest {journey := j, value := v}))) else (ctor Option.none [BudgetRequest])))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

definition{
  name: decodeMotion,
  parameters: [s : Bytes, start : Nat],
  result: (Option MotionRequest),
  body: (let e : Nat := (fieldEnd s start (Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s)); (match (parseNumber (sliceOf s start (prim subtract e start : Nat)))
    | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [MotionRequest])
    | Option.some d => (if (e <? (Foundation.Bytes::byteLength s)) then (match (flagTail s (e + 1))
      | Option.none => (ctor Option.none [MotionRequest])
      | Option.some r => (ctor Option.some [MotionRequest] (record MotionRequest {duration := d, reduced := r}))) else (ctor Option.none [MotionRequest])))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

definition{
  name: dispatch,
